- **anlogic-code-design**: 安路 FPGA 代码设计 skill。Use when: 需要把已分析清楚的需求落成一致风格的 RTL，规范接口、简化 primitive 引脚连接、保持可扩展可移植，并形成交给 anlogic-testbench 的设计交付。 ([folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow](https://github.com/folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow/tree/HEAD/anlogic-code-design) · ★4)
- **anlogic-create-bitstream**: 安路 FPGA bitstream 生成 skill。Use when: 需要执行、排查或审阅 TD 的 bitgen、setup_debugger、export_bitgen_param、export_bid，确认从已放行的 pr.db 到 .bit/.bid/.bitgen_param.f 的边界、输入、交付物和放行条件，判断问题属于 bitgen 参数、前置 pr.db、probe/debugger 还是文件/权限，并避免把 route 或下载交付误塞进 bitstream 阶段。 ([folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow](https://github.com/folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow/tree/HEAD/anlogic-create-bitstream) · ★4)
- **anlogic-modelsim**: Use when Anlogic FPGA verification inputs are ready and Codex needs to run ModelSim, organize `run.do` plus `wave.do`, preserve waveform artifacts, export `.vcd` from `.wlf`, and produce a pass/fail/block conclusion that is valid for downstream waveform extraction and signal comparison. ([folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow](https://github.com/folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow/tree/HEAD/anlogic-modelsim) · ★4)
- **anlogic-needs-analysis**: Use when Anlogic FPGA work still needs requirement decomposition, device and resource judgment, interface analysis, module partitioning, timing/data-flow planning, or upstream design direction. This stage must decide when the requirement is clear enough to hand off either to `anlogic-python-signal-pre-research` for a reference timing baseline or to later specialist Anlogic skills. ([folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow](https://github.com/folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow/tree/HEAD/anlogic-needs-analysis) · ★4)
- **anlogic-place-route**: 安路 FPGA 布局布线 flow skill。Use when: 需要执行、排查或审阅 TD 物理实现阶段的 opt_place、opt_route，确认从 gate.db 到 pr.db 的边界、输入、交付物和放行条件，判断问题属于 place、route、hold 修复、时钟/拥塞/DRC，避免把综合问题或 bitgen 交付误塞进物理实现阶段。 ([folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow](https://github.com/folsie/anlogic-fpga-workflow/tree/HEAD/anlogic-place-route) · ★4)
- **anlogic-project-creation**: Use when Anlogic FPGA verification is already complete and Codex needs to create the TD project from validated design inputs, constraints, IP, and wrappers. This stage begins only after `anlogic-modelsim`, `anlogic-wave-signal-analyzer`, and `anlogic-signal-comparison` have produced a clean release conclusion.
